To the Ephesians, St. Paul speaks: Thus says God, the mystery has been made known to us, willing to do good to his good pleasure, which He purposed in himself, in the dispensation of the fullness of times to gather together in one all things in Christ, both which are in heaven, and which are on earth, even in Him. Ephesians [Garbled] chapter.

St. Jerome interprets the word "Gather together" as opposed to the interpretations of the grammarians, saying: "The Apostles teaches: writing to the Ephesians, God has purposed in the fullness of time to gather all things, both in heaven and on earth, under Christ: Go and be in the Savior."

[Page 732]

1650.

In the Revelation of John: "I am Alpha and Omega, the beginning and the end," which has been rightly preserved in the Latin manuscripts more in gathering than in recapitulation. There are predecessors and interpreters, who interpreted it regarding the gathering, so that all might be made under Christ, both angels and men, Jews and Gentiles. Therefore, it was adjourned on the fifth day. After a sermon, there was a chapter given on the same, which sounds as if saying, "at gathering to see."

Erasmus of Rotterdam wrote in his annotations, "This word of the Apostle was written thus: 'To gather together,' that is to recapitulate, so that it might be comprehended in infinity." For Origen stated 'to gather together,' that is recapitulation, as he directed for posterity. In whose words, among others, this word "Gather together" was explained thus, as reintroduce, renew, reflect. But he states that with a great judgment.

and to those who from the beginning were, to life He will restore, conversing much among men. St. John explains thus: "I saw a new heaven and a new earth, for the first heaven and the first earth passed away and there was no more sea." And I, John, saw the holy city, new Jerusalem, coming down from God out of heaven, prepared as a bride adorned for her husband. And I heard a great voice out of the throne saying, "Behold, the tabernacle of God is with men, and he will dwell with them, and they shall be his people, and God Himself shall be with them, and be their God." And God shall wipe away all tears from their eyes, and there shall be no more death, neither sorrow, nor crying, nor pain anymore: for the former things are passed away." And He that sat upon the throne said, "I am Alpha and Omega, the beginning and the end." Revelation 21: Cap.

Regarding the same mystery Isaiah speaks: "I am sought of them that asked not for me; I am found of those that sought me not," says the Lord. "Behold me, behold me, to a nation that was not called by my name."

Erasmus Roterodamus in annotationibus suis scripser, hoc verba Apostoli sic scripsit. Infinitāre se dāraepādāacod, id est recapituliati, nat ei, vt in finitumam comprehenderent. Nam Oresi, eos voverât dāraepādāacown id est recapitula­tionem, sicut ea, quae posteritatem finxi. Quorum verbis, in annisse, hoc verbum Infinitāre, ās forbredico, sic refereno, renodio, refiexio. Ego voni protracta sereumones Años. Quinetur autem, regni Resfarium, refertit mihi vigoris & vices dicere. 
Et ecce Michael vonius de praeceptibus spirius re nit in aditiorien mueum; et ego reman. ib iusta regenu Resafarium. Veni autem, A doctrina te, quae verique sunt aspir, in noifinis diibus. 
D. Hieronymius R neba haec: An noifinis di bus: ait inspenda else, non in vicios tempores, sed in noifinis diibus, id est, in Consummatione sceli. 
Atque ita Daniel libri thabaecia, deducit a voce Et, id est, Deus, et Dan, hoc est, indiciis, lis causa, contentio. 
Dictio itad Daniel idem sonat, quid Deus ridenti, litis & Contentiosis, non quod Daniel iste horreum vitruum muellis defecit. 
Et quod hoc hic non. Explicavit, non tem situs Dan. sint Antichristi, reiam Aluam priorem, fo mininum mores in noifinis mundi diibus. 
tatos: de quibus Phisicus persic i. Et ves. is pra sentia contestative.

[Page 777]
Romanus, amilanus secilareseu apostolorum ceteris imperati, donec veniat Salutationis illius, quod ecclesia debet. Gene. 49. cap. De dio sa, ultraes roti tempus, ipsum vocabuli Daniel in literis suis numerabilibus, tam materialiter quam formalliter, quando nam id eivorhtum, et Syllabere constitatione. Loquitur meum vir, habilitis lineis, de hac acade Evangelica Ecclesia, ejus sipium esist populus Danielis Propheta in. Habitat Babylonia, ma cum hoc prope. Hic regnum Pope dicitur Civitas magna Babylonia, quia don it populus Danielis, sed, hoc est, reginum, cum hae reverticese Babylonius fornicantium. Judit super. Flans. It: at populus Israeli in ha Babylonia richa raeitis Abresius a transvairs, hoc est. Evangelica ecclesia. Hie dicitit se Danielis: quae fab initio indici ante christum vin & trivialitas, nem posituos. Apocal. 2. Ato igitur misseri, in voc Daniel, si cum signficatis hebraica, cum literas.

Ian solis & mihi sis nomine romanis vt si nventebicis versub. Anno missu 4816 cathderan tom lister. Ioannes 8. Farnina confertit romanam, vt ipsius sit netueribus Babylonica: Ergo cuius Romana senist ab extra liberum, hoc est in litter. Non in iffibus liberis nudius, hoc est, non in interiublinis numeris 2420, qui per mediam Libri vitæ transferet: Quod Libri mei lidium D. Ioannes vocat medium celi, dicens; Et vid eum in Angelorum volatorem per medium celi, habentem T. Angelisium tenorinn, vt evangelitis, et senatoribus ipsius teverans. Et alius Angelus.

Marius confractum quintûm digitum, siue Honorius præcidit, recen­dit de dignis montibus meretriciis Babylonicae, qui decidere, aut cadunt anno Domini 1620, das 17. cap. An plures digito­rum medio meretricis Ba­bilonis sedet, dei digitus vocatur infantis, & flauus. 

Et veteres, quæ adhibere medio digito, indicant, bavi labente significabant eins infimioris, cui­us talonem profter minian obseruationem hi con­trà praesentia: Videntur procrebentur mer­curi in digitum, ostendere.

Atque marius quintum digitorum pre­cisentum fuit in digni­tatibus illis diritis Edinis, sedentis in tardis. Lice 16. Et per illas quinqu ciudades in Aegipto Imperio Romano. Quid sentis de hoc, quòd Angelus dicit: Multi de his, qui donabant in texce puluere euiélábunt: alí in vitam eternam: alí in opprobrium, vt semper vi decent ?

Paciens tibi de his, quæ habemus in hoc libro a me scripta sunt, mea infirmitate & di nomen declarata. S. didi sanctoris sententiae de sacris mysterijs noveris, per me, liberè cante, reliqui, & scripsitis: A. Noititate hac (sci. si) ser­vitia Theolatorium presidisdam, veritas observetur non mirus, quam Sidai, dicente Christo: ante­cedentem Abraham fieret ego sim. Ioan. 8.

Vox Multis, partitis, non totius et: vt cum e­lis ait ad Zachariam, de eius nascituro filius Josue Baptista, Multi in radicibus eius pa­debunt: exit enim magnis coram Domino. Duae 2. c. Multi autem & non omnes hoc fec­e, reinot dicente Christo: Ioannes baptista, nequè man.

Tertius Christi aduentus seau, di, et eius iudicij millenarius.

Christus ait ad Ecclesiam Philadelphia in qua hoc iudicium adueniens feruida celebretur. Eo iudicabo ab hora tentationis, qui ventura in orben nouētibus, tentare habentes inter, ea, apocal. 3. de his se inquit D. Ioannes. Et post hoc (ab anno Domini 590.) protet Satanas man soliti modico tempore 30 annos erunt per fines vt videre est in quinto & septo millenarii. Et vidi fides (idicium.) et sederunt supereras, iudicium datum est illis, et (vidi) animas decollat, torum proster te relinquinian. Et profeter vero, bun Dei, et dei non aduocaretur lapsum, neff imaginem eius: espi, acceperunt charac teria eius in frontibus, aut in manibus suis. hoc est, qui man cum meretricæ Babylonica, iei cum homine
